Appendix E - Glossary of construction and system terms

TermMeaning
Accounting periodA month or similar slice of the financial year that is open for posting or closed.
AddendumA formal change issued by the client during the tender period.
Advance paymentUp-front money from the client to mobilise; recovered from later IPCs; normally guaranteed by a bond.
Anti-exfiltrationControls limiting and watching bulk data export (volume budgets, watermarks, alerts).
AttestationA signature, issued by the isolated signer for an authenticated human session, that the database verifies before accepting a decision.
Automation ruleA configured rule that notifies people or prepares drafts; it can never decide.
Back-chargeA cost recovered from a subcontractor for work or damage the contractor had to deal with.
Ball in courtThe party who currently has to act on an RFI or submittal.
Base unitThe unit in which stock is stored (for example kg); purchase and issue units convert to it.
BaselineThe approved schedule or budget against which progress is measured.
BCFBIM collaboration format for exchanging model-based issues.
Bid bondA guarantee issued with a bid so the client is protected if the winner refuses to sign.
Blanket order / call-offA framework agreement with agreed prices, from which individual deliveries are called off.
BOQBill of quantities: the priced list of work items and quantities that forms the basis of a contract and a budget.
Budget transferMoving approved budget from one cost code to another; total unchanged.
CDECommon data environment: the controlled place where project documents live (ISO 19650).
CertificateThe contractor's approved statement of what is payable, after measurement and deductions.
Change eventA potential or real change (client instruction, design change, delay) tracked from "potential" to "approved" with cost and time impact.
CommitmentA promise to spend backed by an approved purchase order or subcontract; counted against the budget immediately.
ConflictThe server cannot accept a queued command as it was, because the underlying record changed; it is returned to the author with before and after.
ContingencyA budget reserve held for unforeseen cost; drawn down only by approved transfer.
Contract asset / liabilityIFRS 15 balances showing revenue earned but not billed (asset) or billed but not earned (liability).
Control review reportA report of exceptions: segregation-of-duties exceptions, budget overrides, approvals outside SLA, reopened periods and revealed bank details.
Cost codeThe account on which budget, commitments and actual cost are tracked within a project.
Critical pathThe chain of activities that determines the earliest possible finish; zero total float.
Defects liability periodThe period (often 12 months) after completion during which the contractor must fix defects.
Device registrationEnrolling a specific phone to a specific user so the server can recognise and revoke it.
DoADelegation-of-authority matrix: who may approve what, up to which amount.
EAC / ETCEstimate at completion / estimate to complete: expected final cost and expected remaining cost.
Effective-datedA rule that applies from a given date and keeps its history.
EOTExtension of time: contractual relief for delay not caused by the contractor.
EVMEarned value management: PV, EV, AC, SPI, CPI and forecasts that measure project performance objectively.
ExpeditingChasing suppliers to meet delivery dates.
Facts-only gatewayThe assistant interface that gives the model only computed, permission-filtered facts.
Fault injectionDeliberately breaking part of the system (killing a process, cutting the network) to see whether the controls hold.
FIDICInternational standard conditions of contract (Red, Yellow, Silver Books) widely used in construction.
FIFOFirst in, first out: stock valued as if the oldest purchases are used first.
FloatHow long an activity can slip without delaying the project (total float) or its successor (free float).
FragnetA small network of delay events inserted into the schedule to measure the time impact of a change.
Ghost workerA person on the payroll who does not work for the company.
Go/no-goThe decision whether a tender is worth bidding for.
GRNGoods receipt note: the record that goods arrived, in what quantity and condition.
Guarantee / bondA bank or insurer's promise to pay the client if the contractor fails (bid, advance-payment, performance, retention bonds).
Hash chainEach audit record contains a fingerprint of the previous record, so tampering is detectable.
Heat numberThe steel mill's batch identifier for rebar, tied to its test certificate.
Hire rateThe internal (or external) charge per hour, day or kilometre for using a machine.
Hold pointA point in the work beyond which work must not proceed until an inspection is released.
IdempotentDoing the same command twice has the same effect as doing it once, so retries and duplicates are safe.
IFCOpen file format for building models.
ImprestA petty-cash float topped up to a fixed amount against receipts.
Integrity scanA routine that re-computes fingerprints and checks immutability to find out-of-band changes.
IntercompanyTransactions between group companies, eliminated on consolidation.
IPCInterim payment certificate: certified value of work done to date, from which the client pays after retention and advance recovery.
ITPInspection and test plan: the list of inspections, hold points and witness points for an activity.
JournalA balanced set of debit and credit lines recording a financial event.
LagA delay between two linked activities, such as concrete curing time.
Landed costThe full cost of imported goods on arrival: purchase price plus freight, insurance, duty and clearing, spread over the items.
Legal entityA separately registered company within the group, with its own currency, tax registration and books.
Letter of creditA bank's commitment to pay a foreign supplier on presentation of shipping documents.
LTIFRLost-time injury frequency rate: lost-time injuries per million hours worked.
Maker / checkerThe person who prepares a document and a different person who approves it. Also called segregation of duties.
Master dataReference records (projects, suppliers, customers, cost codes, resources) that transactions point to.
MFA / step-upMulti-factor authentication / a fresh MFA challenge required for one specific sensitive action.
NCRNon-conformance report: a record that work or material did not meet requirements, with corrective action.
Near missAn event that could have caused injury or damage but did not.
Offline-firstThe application works fully without a connection and synchronises later.
Original budgetThe budget at baseline, never changed. Shown beside the current (revised) budget.
P50 / P80The cost contingency that will be enough 50% / 80% of the time according to a risk simulation.
pain.001 / camt.053 / MT940Standard bank file formats for payment instructions and account statements.
Pay-when-certifiedA rule that the contractor pays a subcontractor only after the client has certified the same work.
Payment applicationThe subcontractor's monthly claim for work done.
Payment instructionA record telling the bank to pay; it is not proof of payment.
Permit to workA formal authorisation, with conditions and expiry, to do a dangerous task.
PITRPoint-in-time recovery: restoring the database to a chosen moment.
POPurchase order: the controlled commitment to buy.
PostingMaking a journal permanent in the ledger.
PPCPercent plan complete: share of the weekly planned tasks finished.
Practical completionThe date the works are substantially finished and the defects period starts.
Pre-qualificationChecking that a supplier or subcontractor is licensed, insured and tax-compliant before use.
Preventive maintenancePlanned servicing triggered by hours, distance or calendar before a breakdown.
Price adjustmentAdjusting contract payments for changes in labour and material price indices by a formula.
PrincipalAny actor the system recognises: a human, a service or an AI.
Prompt injectionHidden instructions inside content (an invoice, an email) designed to make an AI misbehave.
PV / EV / ACPlanned value / earned value / actual cost: budgeted cost of work scheduled / of work done / actually spent.
QuorumThe number of different approvers required at a workflow step.
RBAC / ABACRole-based / attribute-based access control: permissions from roles, further limited by attributes such as project, amount band or device.
RemeasurementRevaluing foreign-currency balances at the period-end rate (IAS 21).
ReplayRe-sending an old valid request in the hope it is accepted again.
ReservationQuantity set aside for a planned activity so it is not issued elsewhere.
RetentionA percentage withheld from payments as security for defects; released at practical completion and the end of the defects period.
ReversalA new journal that cancels an earlier posted journal; the original is never altered.
RFIRequest for information: a formal question to the designer or consultant, tracked until answered.
RFQRequest for quotation: asking several suppliers to price the same requirement.
RPO / RTORecovery point objective (maximum data loss) / recovery time objective (maximum downtime).
SBOMSoftware bill of materials: list of all components in a build.
Schedule of values (SoV)The breakdown of a subcontract price into items, used to measure progress and certify payments.
SCIMA standard for automatically creating and removing users from the identity provider.
Semantic layerA governed set of business definitions (for example "committed cost") that report builders use, so everyone means the same thing.
SettlementThe bank confirming (on a statement) that the money left or arrived.
SIEMCentral security log and alert system.
Single-source justificationA recorded reason for buying from only one supplier.
Site diaryThe daily record of weather, labour, equipment, work done, delays and instructions.
SLA / escalationA time limit on a step; if missed the task escalates to the next person.
SLO / SLAService level objective / agreement: the measured level of availability or speed promised.
Snag / punch listThe list of small defects to fix before handover.
SoDSegregation of duties: preparing, approving and posting are done by different people.
SPI / CPISchedule performance index EV/PV and cost performance index EV/AC. Below 1.0 is behind schedule / over cost.
SRSSoftware Requirements Specification: the agreed list of what the system shall do and the qualities it shall have.
SSOSingle sign-on through the company identity provider (OpenID Connect or SAML).
Stock countPhysical counting of stock and comparing with the system; differences become adjustments.
SubledgerDetailed ledger (payables, receivables, stock, fixed assets) that must equal one control account in the general ledger.
SubmittalA document (shop drawing, material sample) the contractor submits for approval before using it.
SyncSending queued commands from the phone to the server and receiving results.
Take-offMeasuring quantities from drawings or a model to fill the BOQ.
TelematicsMachine data (hours, fuel, location) sent automatically by the machine.
TenantOne construction company using the product, completely isolated from every other company.
Three-way matchChecking a supplier invoice against the purchase order and the goods receipt before it is approved for payment.
Time barA contractual deadline after which a claim right is lost, for example a 28-day notice period.
TINTax identification number of a business partner.
TransmittalA formal cover record of documents sent to named recipients, who acknowledge receipt.
TRIRTotal recordable incident rate, per 200,000 or 1,000,000 hours depending on the tenant standard.
UATUser acceptance testing: business users confirm the system does their job as agreed, before go-live.
Unit rateThe price for one unit of an item of work, built up from labour, plant, material and subcontract costs plus overhead, risk and margin.
VariationA change to the work ordered by the client that changes the contract price and/or time.
WACWeighted average cost: stock valued at the running average of purchase prices.
WastageMaterial used beyond the theoretical quantity in the BOQ.
WBSWork breakdown structure: the hierarchical breakdown of the project into manageable parts, each with cost codes.
WCAGWeb content accessibility guidelines.
WIPWork in progress schedule showing revenue earned vs billed, and so over- or under-billing.
Withholding taxTax deducted from a payment to a supplier or subcontractor and paid to the tax authority; rate per the signed tax fixture.
